```
1	// 原版 pass 15 "Generate Ice Biome"(WorldGen.cs:5372)移植。
2	// 以 Reset 掷的 snowOriginLeft/Right(=地牢 X ± 扩展)为起点,逐行随机走左右边界;
3	// num2 行以上整体转换(土/草/腐草/粘土/沙→雪 147,石→冰 161,土墙→雪墙 40),
4	// num2 行以下按随机走厚度 num5(0-50)向下转换。记录 snowMinX/snowMaxX/snowBottom。
5	import type { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
6	import type { RNG } from '../../../core/rng';
7	import type { GenState } from './GenState';
8	import { T, TILE_BY_KEY } from '../../../data/tiles';
9	
10	export function runIceBiomePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
11	  const { w, h } = st;
12	  gs.snowTop = gs.worldSurface;
13	  const num2 = gs.lavaLine - rng.int(160, 199); // 完整转换深度线
14	  let left = gs.snowOriginLeft, right = gs.snowOriginRight;
15	  let thickness = 10;
16	  gs.snowMinX = new Float32Array(h);
17	  gs.snowMaxX = new Float32Array(h);
18	
19	  const CORRUPT_GRASS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_23_corrupt_grass_block']!;
20	  const CLAY = TILE_BY_KEY['clay']!;
21	  const ICE = TILE_BY_KEY['ice']!;
22	
23	  for (let y = 0; y <= gs.lavaLine - 140; y++) {
24	    left += rng.int(-4, 3);
25	    right += rng.int(-3, 4);
26	    if (y > 0) {
27	      left = (left + gs.snowMinX[y - 1]) / 2;
28	      right = (right + gs.snowMaxX[y - 1]) / 2;
29	    }
30	    // 雪原整体向地牢侧缓慢漂移
31	    if (gs.dungeonSide > 0) {
32	      if (rng.next() * 4 < 1) { left++; right++; }
33	    } else if (rng.next() * 4 < 1) { left--; right--; }
34	    gs.snowMinX[y] = left;
35	    gs.snowMaxX[y] = right;
36	    const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(left)), x1 = Math.min(w, Math.ceil(right));
37	    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
38	      if (y < num2) {
39	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
40	        // 原版只写 type 不激活——未激活格保持空气,跳过
41	        if (!st.flags[i]) continue;
42	        if (st.wall[i] === 2) st.wall[i] = 40;
43	        const t = st.type[i];
44	        if (t === T.DIRT || t === T.GRASS || t === CORRUPT_GRASS || t === CLAY || t === T.SAND) st.type[i] = T.SNOW;
45	        else if (t === T.STONE) st.type[i] = ICE;
46	      } else {
47	        thickness += rng.int(-3, 3);
48	        if (rng.next() * 3 < 1) {
49	          thickness += rng.int(-4, 4);
50	          if (rng.next() * 3 < 1) thickness += rng.int(-6, 6);
51	        }
52	        if (thickness < 0) thickness = rng.int(0, 2);
53	        else if (thickness > 50) thickness = 50 - rng.int(0, 2);
54	        for (let y2 = y; y2 < y + thickness && y2 < h; y2++) {
55	          const i = st.idx(x, y2);
56	          if (!st.flags[i]) continue;
57	          if (st.wall[i] === 2) st.wall[i] = 40;
58	          const t = st.type[i];
59	          if (t === T.DIRT || t === T.GRASS || t === CORRUPT_GRASS || t === CLAY || t === T.SAND) st.type[i] = T.SNOW;
60	          else if (t === T.STONE) st.type[i] = ICE;
61	        }
62	      }
63	    }
64	    if (gs.snowBottom < y) gs.snowBottom = y;
65	  }
66	}
67
```

```
1	// 原版 WorldGen.SpreadGrass 移植(WorldGen.cs:45236)——生成期草皮传播。
2	// 泥/石完全被包住(3×3 全实心)不转;岩浆邻接不转;向日葵上方不转腐化/猩红/神圣草。
3	import type { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
4	import { T, TILE_BY_KEY, TILE_DEFS } from '../../../data/tiles';
5	
6	/** 把 (x,y) 及相邻同源 tile 转 grass 类型;repeat=true 时 BFS 式向四周扩散 */
7	export function spreadGrass(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, dirt: number, grass: number, repeat = true, depth = 0): void {
8	  if (depth > 400) return;
9	  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
10	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
11	  if (st.type[i] !== dirt) return;
12	  // 完全封闭(3×3 全实心)不转——原版 flag
13	  let enclosed = true;
14	  for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1 && enclosed; dx++) {
15	    for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1 && enclosed; dy++) {
16	      if (dx === 0 && dy === 0) continue;
17	      const j = st.idx(x + dx, y + dy);
18	      if (st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy) && (!st.flags[j] || !TILE_DEFS[st.type[j]]?.solid)) enclosed = false;
19	    }
20	  }
21	  if (enclosed) return;
22	  st.type[i] = grass;
23	  if (!repeat) return;
24	  for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
25	    for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
26	      if (dx === 0 && dy === 0) continue;
27	      const nx = x + dx, ny = y + dy;
28	      if (!st.inBounds(nx, ny)) continue;
29	      const ni = st.idx(nx, ny);
30	      if (st.type[ni] === dirt) spreadGrass(st, nx, ny, dirt, grass, true, depth + 1);
31	    }
32	  }
33	}
34	
35	/** 全图传播(对应原版逐 tile 调用 SpreadGrass 的 pass,如 Mud Caves To Grass)。
36	 * centerX/maxDist 可选:仅转换距中心 maxDist 内的泥(参考世界正是泥迹全图、
37	 * 丛林草紧贴丛林核心——远离核心的泥保持泥色) */
38	export function spreadGrassAll(st: TileStore, dirt: number, grass: number, centerX = -1, maxDist = 0): void {
39	  for (let y = 0; y < st.h; y++) {
40	    for (let x = 0; x < st.w; x++) {
41	      if (centerX >= 0 && Math.abs(x - centerX) > maxDist) continue;
42	      if (st.type[st.idx(x, y)] === dirt) spreadGrass(st, x, y, dirt, grass, false);
43	    }
44	  }
45	  // 单格不扩散传播力弱,重复两轮让草皮连通。
46	  // 关键:必须以"轮初快照"判定邻草——同轮内边扫边转会在扫描方向上链式雪崩,
47	  // 把整条连通泥网全部转成草(丛林"全草无泥"与远程草带的根因)
48	  for (let round = 0; round < 2; round++) {
49	    const targets: number[] = [];
50	    for (let y = 0; y < st.h; y++) {
51	      for (let x = 0; x < st.w; x++) {
52	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
53	        if (st.type[i] !== dirt) continue;
54	        let touch = false;
55	        for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1 && !touch; dx++) {
56	          for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
57	            if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy)) continue;
58	            if (st.type[st.idx(x + dx, y + dy)] === grass) { touch = true; break; }
59	          }
60	        }
61	        if (touch) targets.push(i);
62	      }
63	    }
64	    for (const i of targets) st.type[i] = grass;
65	  }
66	  void T; void TILE_BY_KEY;
67	}
68
```

```
2	// 岩层深处放 numMushroomBiomes 个蘑菇生态:ShroomPatch 泥 blob(墙 80 蘑菇),
3	// 随后泥 59→蘑菇草 70 全图传播(我们的 spreadGrassAll 近似原版逐 tile SpreadGrass)。
4	import type { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
5	import type { RNG } from '../../../core/rng';
6	import type { GenState } from './GenState';
7	import { TILE_BY_KEY } from '../../../data/tiles';
8	import { tileRunner } from './TileRunner';
9	import { spreadGrassAll } from './Spread';
10	
11	const MUD = TILE_BY_KEY['mud']!;
12	const JUNGLE_GRASS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_60_jungle_grass_block']!;
13	const MUSH_GRASS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_70_mushroom_grass_block']!;
14	const SNOW = TILE_BY_KEY['snow']!;
15	const ICE = TILE_BY_KEY['ice']!;
16	const THIN_ICE = TILE_BY_KEY['thin_ice']!;
17	const GRANITE = TILE_BY_KEY['v_368_granite_block']!;
18	const MARBLE = TILE_BY_KEY['v_367_marble_block']!;
19	
20	export function runMushroomPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
21	  const { w, h } = st;
22	  const van = w / 4200;
23	  const maxBiomes = Math.min(Math.floor(w / 700), 13);
24	  const placed: Array<[number, number]> = [];
25	
26	  for (let n = 0; n < maxBiomes; n++) {
27	    let attempts = 0;
28	    while (attempts <= Math.floor(w / 2)) {
29	      let i = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.2), Math.floor(w * 0.8) - 1);
30	      if (attempts > Math.floor(w / 4)) {
31	        i = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.25), Math.floor(w * 0.975) - 1);
32	      }
33	      const j = rng.int(gs.rockLevel + 50, h - 301);
34	      // 区域检查:避开雪/冰/丛林/花岗岩/大理石与已放蘑菇(原版 100/500 距离)
35	      let bad = false;
36	      for (let x = i - 100; x < i + 100 && !bad; x += 3) {
37	        for (let y = j - 100; y < j + 100; y += 3) {
38	          if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) { bad = true; break; }
39	          const t = st.type[st.idx(x, y)];
40	          if (t === SNOW || t === ICE || t === THIN_ICE || t === JUNGLE_GRASS || t === GRANITE || t === MARBLE) { bad = true; break; }
41	        }
42	      }
43	      if (!bad) {
44	        for (const [px, py] of placed) {
45	          if (Math.hypot(px - i, py - j) < 500) { bad = true; break; }
46	        }
47	      }
48	      if (!bad && placed.length < maxBiomes) {
49	        shroomPatch(st, rng, gs, i, j, van);
50	        for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) shroomPatch(st, rng, gs, i + rng.int(-40, 40), j + rng.int(-40, 40), van);
51	        placed.push([i, j]);
52	        break;
53	      }
54	      attempts++;
55	    }
56	  }
57	  // 泥→蘑菇草传播
58	  spreadGrassAll(st, MUD, MUSH_GRASS);
59	}
60	
61	/** ShroomPatch(WorldGen.cs:48648)1:1:泥 blob 随机走,中心上空挖空,墙→80,尾端泥 runner */
62	function shroomPatch(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, j: number, van: number): void {
63	  let stepsLeft = rng.int(80, 99);
64	  let radius = rng.int(20, 25) * van;
65	  const startR = radius;
66	  let px = i + 0.0, py = j - radius * 0.3;
67	  let vx = rng.int(-100, 100) * 0.005;
68	  let vy = rng.int(-200, -101) * 0.005;
69	  while (stepsLeft > 0 && radius > 0) {
70	    stepsLeft -= rng.int(0, 2);
71	    radius--;
72	    const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(px - stepsLeft * 0.5));
73	    const x1 = Math.min(st.w, Math.floor(px + stepsLeft * 0.5));
74	    const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(py - stepsLeft * 0.5));
75	    const y1 = Math.min(st.h, Math.floor(py + stepsLeft * 0.5));
76	    const r = stepsLeft * rng.int(80, 119) * 0.01;
77	    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
78	      for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
79	        const d = Math.hypot(x - px, (y - py) * 2.3);
80	        const ti = st.idx(x, y);
81	        if (d < r * 0.8 && st.liquidType[ti] === 2) st.liquid[ti] = 0;
82	        if (d < r * 0.2 && y < py) {
83	          st.type[ti] = 0; st.flags[ti] = 0;
84	          if (st.wall[ti] > 0) st.wall[ti] = 80;
85	        } else if (d < r * 0.4 * (0.95 + rng.next() * 0.1)) {
86	          st.type[ti] = MUD;
87	          if (radius === startR && y > py) st.flags[ti] = 1;
88	          if (st.wall[ti] > 0) st.wall[ti] = 80;
89	        }
90	      }
91	    }
92	    px += vx; py += vy;
93	    px += vx;
94	    vx += rng.int(-100, 109) * 0.005;
95	    vy -= rng.int(0, 109) * 0.005;
96	    if (vx > -0.5 && vx < 0.5) vx = vx >= 0 ? 0.5 : -0.5;
97	    vx = Math.max(-0.5, Math.min(0.5, vx));
98	    vy = Math.max(-0.5, Math.min(0.5, vy));
99	    // 尾端两支向下泥 runner
100	    for (let k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
101	      let i1 = Math.floor(px) + rng.int(-19, 19);
102	      let j1 = Math.floor(py) + rng.int(0, 19);
103	      let found = false;
104	      for (let a = 0; a < 30; a++) {
105	        if (st.inBounds(i1, j1) && (st.flags[st.idx(i1, j1)] || st.type[st.idx(i1, j1)] === MUD)) { found = true; break; }
106	        i1 = Math.floor(px) + rng.int(-19, 19);
107	        j1 = Math.floor(py) + rng.int(0, 19);
108	      }
109	      if (!found) continue;
110	      tileRunner(st, rng, gs, i1, j1, { strength: rng.int(10, 19), steps: rng.int(10, 19), type: MUD, speedY: 2, noYChange: true });
111	    }
112	  }
113	}
114	

```

```
2	// 大理石:板格穹顶洞窟——宽 Next(80,150)/3×3 = 78-149 tile,高 39-57;
3	// 上半中空(墙 178)+下半实心大理石(367),边缘毛化。

6	import type { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
7	import type { RNG } from '../../../core/rng';
8	import type { GenState } from './GenState';
9	import { T, TILE_BY_KEY } from '../../../data/tiles';

16	const SANDSTONE = TILE_BY_KEY['sandstone']!, HARDSAND = TILE_BY_KEY['hardened_sand']!;

18	export function runMarbleGranitePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
19	  const count = Math.max(2, Math.round(st.w / 700));
20	  // 大理石带:xPad 80;花岗岩带:xPad 100
21	  placeMarbleCaves(st, rng, gs, count, 80);
22	  placeGraniteCaves(st, rng, gs, count, 100);
23	}
24	
25	function biomeClear(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
26	  // BiomeTileCheck 简化:半径 30 内无冰雪/丛林/沙漠材料
27	  for (let dx = -30; dx <= 30; dx += 5) {
28	    for (let dy = -30; dy <= 30; dy += 5) {
29	      if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy)) continue;
30	      const t = st.type[st.idx(x + dx, y + dy)];
31	      if (t === SNOW || t === ICE || t === JUNGLE_GRASS || t === SANDSTONE || t === HARDSAND) return false;
32	    }
33	  }
34	  return true;
35	}
36	
37	/** 大理石穹顶洞窟(MarbleBiome.Place 主体流程简化) */
38	function placeMarbleCaves(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, count: number, xPad: number): void {
39	  for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {
40	    // 横向扫过分布
41	    let x = Math.floor((n / count) * (st.w - 2 * xPad)) + xPad + rng.int(-20, 20);
42	    while (x > st.w * 0.45 && x < st.w * 0.55) x = rng.int(gs.beachLeftEnd, st.w - gs.beachLeftEnd - 1);
43	    x = Math.max(xPad, Math.min(st.w - xPad, x));
44	    const y = rng.int(gs.rockLevel + 20, Math.max(gs.rockLevel + 21, st.h - 240));
45	    if (!biomeClear(st, x, y)) continue;
46	    // 尺寸(MarbleBiome):宽 78-149,高 39-57
47	    const halfW = Math.floor((rng.int(80, 149) / 3) * 3 / 2);   // 39-74
48	    const fullH = Math.floor((rng.int(40, 59) / 3) * 3);         // 39-57
49	    const halfH = Math.floor(fullH / 2);
50	    const oy = Math.max(4, y - halfH);
51	    // 椭圆穹顶放置
52	    for (let dx = -halfW; dx <= halfW; dx++) {
53	      const chord = Math.floor(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, 1 - (dx / halfW) ** 2)) * halfH);
54	      if (chord <= 0) continue;
55	      for (let dy = -chord; dy <= chord; dy++) {
56	        const px = x + dx, py = oy + halfH + dy;
57	        if (!st.inBounds(px, py)) continue;
58	        const i = st.idx(px, py);
59	        // 上半(含中线以上 40%)= 洞窟:墙 178,保留原 tile 但清实心块?原版腔内 ClearEverything
60	        if (dy < -chord * 0.2) {
61	          st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0;
62	          st.wall[i] = 178;
63	        } else if (dy > chord * 0.15 || rng.next() < 0.4) {
64	          // 下半 = 实心大理石(矿石保留由 flag3 语义覆盖,这里直接覆盖)
65	          st.type[i] = MARBLE; st.flags[i] = 1;
66	          if (dy < chord * 0.3) st.wall[i] = 178;
67	        }
68	      }
69	      // 边缘毛化:两端列 50% 跳过
70	      if ((dx === -halfW || dx === halfW) && rng.next() < 0.5) continue;
71	    }
72	  }
73	}
74	
75	/** 花岗岩岩浆体(GraniteBiome.Place 简化:200×200 区大实心体 + 蜿蜒洞) */
76	function placeGraniteCaves(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, count: number, xPad: number): void {
77	  for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {
78	    let x = Math.floor((n / count) * (st.w - 2 * xPad)) + xPad + rng.int(-20, 20);
79	    while (x > st.w * 0.45 && x < st.w * 0.55) x = rng.int(gs.beachLeftEnd, st.w - gs.beachLeftEnd - 1);
80	    x = Math.max(xPad, Math.min(st.w - xPad, x));
81	    const y = rng.int(gs.rockLevel + 20, Math.max(gs.rockLevel + 21, st.h - 240));
82	    if (!biomeClear(st, x, y)) continue;
83	    // 大实心花岗岩体(半径 ~55-75),矿石保留:用 tileRunner(石族只换石头)
84	    const radius = rng.int(55, 75);
85	    tileRunner(st, rng, gs, x, y, { strength: radius * 2, steps: 30, type: GRANITE });
86	    // 墙 180 涂抹
87	    for (let dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx += 2) {
88	      for (let dy = -radius; dy <= radius; dy += 2) {
89	        if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy)) continue;
90	        const i = st.idx(x + dx, y + dy);
91	        if (st.type[i] === GRANITE && dx * dx + dy * dy < radius * radius) st.wall[i] = 180;
92	      }
93	    }
94	    // 岩浆洞:2-3 条蜿蜒隧道穿体(深于 lavaLine-30 用岩浆)
95	    const deep = y > gs.lavaLine - 30;
96	    for (let k = 0; k < rng.int(2, 3); k++) {
97	      const dx = rng.int(-100, 100) * 0.01, dy = rng.int(-100, 100) * 0.01;
98	      digTunnel(st, rng, x + rng.int(-radius / 2, radius / 2), y + rng.int(-radius / 2, radius / 2),
99	        dx, dy, rng.int(30, 60), rng.int(3, 6), deep && rng.next() < 0.5);
100	    }
101	    // 孤块清理:3×3 实心邻 <3 的花岗岩移除
102	    for (let dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx++) {
103	      for (let dy = -radius; dy <= radius; dy++) {
104	        if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy)) continue;
105	        const i = st.idx(x + dx, y + dy);
106	        if (st.type[i] !== GRANITE || !st.flags[i]) continue;
107	        let n = 0;
108	        for (let ax = -1; ax <= 1; ax++) for (let ay = -1; ay <= 1; ay++) {
109	          const j = st.idx(x + dx + ax, y + dy + ay);
110	          if (st.inBounds(x + dx + ax, y + dy + ay) && st.flags[j]) n++;
111	        }
112	        if (n < 3) { st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0; st.wall[i] = 180; }
113	      }
114	    }
115	  }
116	}
```

```
1	// 原版 JunglePass 移植(GameContent/Biomes/JunglePass.cs,1:1)。
2	// 起点 (jungleOriginX, (h+rockLayer)/2):三轮随机移动 + 泥 runner(带宝石 63/65/67),
3	// 巨型泥 runner(num2=400-600×scale,steps 10000,向上 -20)+ 地表隧道,
4	// 泥墙挖洞(简化)+ 收尾(泥簇/石囊/宝石)。mudWall 期间铺丛林天然墙 64。
5	import type { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
6	import type { RNG } from '../../../core/rng';
7	import type { GenState } from './GenState';
8	import { T, TILE_BY_KEY } from '../../../data/tiles';
9	import { tileRunner } from './TileRunner';
10	
11	const MUD = TILE_BY_KEY['mud']!;
12	const GEMS = ['gem_sapphire', 'gem_ruby', 'gem_emerald', 'gem_topaz', 'gem_amethyst', 'gem_diamond'].map((k) => TILE_BY_KEY[k]!);
13	
14	export function runJunglePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): number {
15	  const { w, h } = st;
16	  const scale = (w / 4200) * 1.5;
17	  const van = w / 4200;
18	  let x = gs.jungleX;
19	  let y = Math.floor((h + gs.rockLevel) / 2);
20	  let zeroX = 0, zeroY = 0;
21	
22	  const applyRandomMovement = (xRange: number, yRange: number) => {
23	    x += rng.int(Math.floor(-xRange * scale), Math.floor(xRange * scale));
24	    y += rng.int(Math.floor(-yRange * scale), Math.floor(yRange * scale));
25	    y = Math.max(gs.rockLevel, Math.min(h - 1, y));
26	  };
27	  const placeFirstPassMud = (xSpeedScale: number) => {
28	    const str = rng.int(Math.floor(250 * scale), Math.floor(500 * scale));
29	    // 原版步数固定 50-150 不缩放(speedX=±3 → 漂移 ≤450;4200 宽的 10.7%)
30	    const stp = rng.int(50, 149);
31	    tileRunner(st, rng, gs, x, y, { strength: str, steps: stp, type: MUD, speedX: gs.dungeonSide * xSpeedScale, speedY: 0, mudWall: true });
32	  };
33	  const placeGemsAt = (baseGem: number, variants: number) => {
34	    for (let n = 0; n < 6 * scale; n++) {
35	      tileRunner(st, rng, gs,
36	        x + rng.int(-Math.floor(125 * scale), Math.floor(125 * scale)),
37	        y + rng.int(-Math.floor(125 * scale), Math.floor(125 * scale)), {
38	          strength: rng.int(3, 6), steps: rng.int(3, 7),
39	          type: GEMS[baseGem + rng.int(0, variants - 1)],
40	        });
41	    }
42	  };
43	
44	  applyRandomMovement(100, 100);
45	  zeroX += x; zeroY += y;
46	  placeFirstPassMud(3);
47	  placeGemsAt(0, 2); // 蓝宝石 63
48	  applyRandomMovement(250, 150);
49	  zeroX += x; zeroY += y;
50	  placeFirstPassMud(0);
51	  placeGemsAt(2, 2); // 祖母绿 65
52	  const oldX = x, oldY = y;
53	  applyRandomMovement(400, 150);
54	  zeroX += x; zeroY += y;
55	  placeFirstPassMud(-3);
56	  placeGemsAt(4, 2); // 黄玉 67
57	
58	  // 巨型泥 runner:三次移动点的均值为中心
59	  const cx = Math.floor(zeroX / 3), cy = Math.floor(zeroY / 3);
60	  const num2 = rng.int(Math.floor(400 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale));
61	  const num3 = Math.floor(25 * scale);
62	  const i = Math.max(gs.beachLeftEnd + Math.floor(num2 / 2) + num3, Math.min(gs.beachRightStart - Math.floor(num2 / 2) - num3, cx));
63	  tileRunner(st, rng, gs, i, cy, { strength: num2, steps: 10000, type: MUD, speedY: -20, mudWall: true });
64	  // 泥柱中心记录给后续(丛林表面草化)
65	  (gs as any).jungleCoreX = i;
66	  const jungleXOut = generateTunnelToSurface(st, rng, gs, i, cy);
67	  generateHolesInMudWalls(st, rng, gs);
68	  generateFinishingTouches(st, rng, gs, oldX, oldY, scale, van);
69	  return jungleXOut;
70	}
71	
72	function generateTunnelToSurface(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, j: number): number {
73	  let radius = rng.int(5, 10);
74	  let px = i + 0.0, py = j + 0.0;
75	  let vx = rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.1;
76	  let vy = rng.int(10, 19) * 0.1;
77	  let count = 0;
78	  let running = true;
79	  let jungleX = i;
80	  while (running) {
81	    if (py < gs.worldSurface) {
82	      const cx = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.w - 10, Math.floor(px)));
83	      const cy = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.h - 10, Math.floor(py)));
84	      // 隧道顶到达无墙无块的开放区即停(检查上方 1-5 格)
85	      let open = true;
86	      for (let d = 0; d <= 5; d++) {
87	        const ii = st.idx(cx, Math.max(5, cy - d));
88	        if (st.wall[ii] !== 0 || st.flags[ii]) { open = false; break; }
89	      }
90	      if (open) running = false;
91	    }
92	    jungleX = Math.floor(px);
93	    radius += rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.1;
94	    if (radius < 5) radius = 5;
95	    if (radius > 10) radius = 10;
96	    const x0 = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.w - 10, Math.floor(px - radius * 0.5)));
97	    const x1 = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.w - 10, Math.floor(px + radius * 0.5)));
98	    const y0 = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.h - 10, Math.floor(py - radius * 0.5)));
99	    const y1 = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.h - 10, Math.floor(py + radius * 0.5)));
100	    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
101	      for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
102	        if (Math.abs(x - px) + Math.abs(y - py) < radius * 0.5 * (1 + rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.015)) {
103	          st.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
104	        }
105	      }
106	    }
107	    count++;
108	    // 偶发分支洞穴
109	    if (count > 10 && rng.int(0, 49) < count) {
110	      count = 0;
111	      const side = rng.next() < 0.5 ? -2 : 2;
112	      tileRunner(st, rng, gs, Math.floor(px), Math.floor(py), {
113	        strength: rng.int(3, 19), steps: rng.int(10, 99), type: -1, speedX: side,
114	      });
115	    }
116	    px += vx; py += vy;
117	    vy += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.01;
118	    if (vy > 0) vy = 0;
119	    if (vy < -2) vy = -2;
120	    vx += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.1;
121	    if (px < i - 200) vx += rng.int(5, 20) * 0.1;
122	    if (px > i + 200) vx -= rng.int(5, 20) * 0.1;
123	    if (vx > 1.5) vx = 1.5;
124	    if (vx < -1.5) vx = -1.5;
125	    if (py < -50 || py > st.h + 50) running = false; // 安全阀
126	  }
127	  return jungleX;
128	}
129	
130	/** 泥墙挖洞(原版 MudWallRunner 的简化:在丛林墙上随机游走清出通道) */
131	function generateHolesInMudWalls(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
132	  const tries = Math.floor(st.w / 4);
133	  for (let n = 0; n < tries; n++) {
134	    let x = rng.int(20, st.w - 21);
135	    let y = rng.int(gs.worldSurface + 10, st.h - 150);
136	    let found = false;
137	    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 40; attempt++) {
138	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
139	      if (st.wall[i] === 64 || st.wall[i] === 15) { found = true; break; }
140	      x = rng.int(20, st.w - 21);
141	      y = rng.int(gs.worldSurface + 10, st.h - 150);
142	    }
143	    if (!found) continue;
144	    // 简化随机走:清墙 30-80 步
145	    let steps = rng.int(30, 80);
146	    while (steps-- > 0) {
147	      if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) break;
148	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
149	      if (st.wall[i] === 64 || st.wall[i] === 15) st.wall[i] = 0;
150	      x += rng.int(-1, 1);
151	      y += rng.int(-1, 1);
152	    }
153	  }
154	}
155	
156	function generateFinishingTouches(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, oldX: number, oldY: number, scale: number, van: number): void {
157	  let x = oldX, y = oldY;
158	  // 大泥簇
159	  for (let n = 0; n <= 20 * scale; n++) {
160	    x += rng.int(Math.floor(-5 * scale), Math.floor(5 * scale));
161	    y += rng.int(Math.floor(-5 * scale), Math.floor(5 * scale));
162	    // 原版 300-500 步固定不缩放
163	    const str = rng.int(40, 99), stp = rng.int(300, 499);
164	    tileRunner(st, rng, gs, x, y, { strength: str, steps: stp, type: MUD });
165	  }
166	  // 洞穴(1/7 填液体)
167	  for (let n = 0; n <= 10 * scale; n++) {
168	    let i2 = oldX + rng.int(-Math.floor(600 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale));
169	    let j2 = oldY + rng.int(-Math.floor(200 * scale), Math.floor(200 * scale));
170	    let found = false;
171	    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 40; attempt++) {
172	      if (i2 >= 1 && i2 < st.w - 1 && j2 >= 1 && j2 < st.h - 1 && st.type[st.idx(i2, j2)] === MUD) { found = true; break; }
173	      i2 = oldX + rng.int(-Math.floor(600 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale));
174	      j2 = oldY + rng.int(-Math.floor(200 * scale), Math.floor(200 * scale));
175	    }
176	    if (!found) continue;
177	    for (let k = 0; k < 8 * scale; k++) {
178	      i2 += rng.int(-30, 30);
179	      j2 += rng.int(-30, 30);
180	      tileRunner(st, rng, gs, i2, j2, {
181	        strength: rng.int(10, 19), steps: rng.int(30, 69),
182	        type: rng.next() * 7 < 1 ? -2 : -1,
183	      });
184	    }
185	  }
186	  // 泥内石囊 + 宝石
187	  for (let n = 0; n <= 300 * scale; n++) {
188	    let i2 = oldX + rng.int(-Math.floor(600 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale));
189	    let j2 = oldY + rng.int(-Math.floor(200 * scale), Math.floor(200 * scale));
190	    let found = false;
191	    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 40; attempt++) {
192	      if (i2 >= 1 && i2 < st.w - 1 && j2 >= 1 && j2 < st.h - 1 && st.type[st.idx(i2, j2)] === MUD) { found = true; break; }
193	      i2 = oldX + rng.int(-Math.floor(600 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale));
194	      j2 = oldY + rng.int(-Math.floor(200 * scale), Math.floor(200 * scale));
195	    }
196	    if (!found) continue;
197	    tileRunner(st, rng, gs, i2, j2, { strength: rng.int(4, 9), steps: rng.int(5, 29), type: T.STONE });
198	    if (rng.next() * 4 < 1) {
199	      tileRunner(st, rng, gs,
200	        i2 + rng.int(-1, 1), j2 + rng.int(-1, 1),
201	        { strength: rng.int(3, 6), steps: rng.int(4, 7), type: GEMS[rng.int(0, 5)] });
202	    }
203	  }
204	}
```

```
179	function isTileNearby(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, type: number, radius: number): boolean {
180	  for (let dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx++) {
181	    for (let dy = -radius; dy <= radius; dy++) {
182	      if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy)) continue;
183	      if (st.type[st.idx(x + dx, y + dy)] === type) return true;
184	    }

189	/** 3×2 物件放置(锚点=中下):帧 style*54 + col*18 / row*18 */
190	function place3x2(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, type: number, style: number): void {
191	  if (x < 5 || x > st.w - 5 || y < 5 || y > st.h - 5) return;
192	  for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
193	    if (!st.isSolid(x + dx, y + 1)) return; // 底座须实心
194	  }
195	  for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
196	    for (let dy = -1; dy <= 0; dy++) {
197	      if (st.flags[st.idx(x + dx, y + dy)]) return; // 区域须净空
198	    }
199	  }
200	  for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
201	    for (let dy = -1; dy <= 0; dy++) {
202	      st.setTileSilent(x + dx, y + dy, type, style * 54 + (dx + 1) * 18, (dy + 1) * 18);
203	    }
204	  }
205	}

207	/** 暗影之球(AddShadowOrb L16035):2×2,猩红 style 偏移 +36 */
208	function addShadowOrb(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, crimson: boolean): void {
209	  if (x < 10 || x > st.w - 10 || y < 10 || y > st.h - 10) return;
210	  for (let dx = -1; dx < 1; dx++) {
211	    for (let dy = -1; dy < 1; dy++) {
212	      if (st.flags[st.idx(x + dx, y + dy)] && st.type[st.idx(x + dx, y + dy)] === ORB) return;
213	    }

215	  const fx = crimson ? 36 : 0;
216	  st.setTileSilent(x - 1, y - 1, ORB, fx, 0);
217	  st.setTileSilent(x, y - 1, ORB, 18 + fx, 0);
218	  st.setTileSilent(x - 1, y, ORB, fx, 18);
219	  st.setTileSilent(x, y, ORB, 18 + fx, 18);

222	/** ChasmRunner(L45779)1:1:竖直裂隙,黑檀石壳 + 球/祭坛收尾 */
223	function chasmRunner(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, j: number, steps: number, makeOrb: boolean): void {
224	  let sideways = false, orbPlaced = !makeOrb, altarPlaced = false;
225	  let num1 = steps;

250	      for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {

252	          const ti = st.idx(x, y);

254	            && st.type[ti] !== ORB) {

256	          }

258	      }

260	    if (num1 <= 2 && py < gs.worldSurface + 45) num1 = 2;
261	    if (num1 <= 0) {
262	      if (!orbPlaced) {
263	        orbPlaced = true;
264	        addShadowOrb(st, Math.floor(px), Math.floor(py), false);
265	      } else if (!altarPlaced) {
266	        altarPlaced = true;
267	        let tries = 0, done = false;
268	        while (!done) {

271	          x = Math.max(5, Math.min(st.w - 5, x));
272	          y = Math.max(5, Math.min(st.h - 5, y));
273	          if (y > gs.worldSurface) {
274	            // 落点吸附(原版):空气则落到地面,实心则升到表面
275	            if (!st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) {
276	              while (!st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] && y < st.h - 2) y++;
277	              y--;
278	            } else {
279	              while (st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] && y > 4) y--;
280	            }
281	            if (!isTileNearby(st, x, y, ALTAR, 3)) place3x2(st, x, y, ALTAR, 0);
282	            if (st.type[st.idx(x, y)] === ALTAR) done = true;
283	            else if (++tries >= 10000) done = true;

285	        }

292	    const x0 = Math.max(1, Math.floor(px - radius * 1.1)), x1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, Math.floor(px + radius * 1.1));
293	    const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(py - radius * 1.1)), y1 = Math.min(st.h, Math.floor(py + radius * 1.1));

394	function crimStart(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, j: number): void {
395	  const entryX = i, entryY0 = Math.min(j, gs.worldSurface);
396	  let py = entryY0;

398	  let px = i + 0.0;

400	  let vx = rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.1;
401	  let vy = rng.int(20, 200) * 0.01;

404	  while (true) {

408	    const y0 = Math.floor(py - radius / 2), y1 = Math.floor(py + radius / 2);

410	      for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
411	        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
412	        const d = Math.abs(x - px) + Math.abs(y - py);
413	        const ti = st.idx(x, y);
414	        if (y > startY) {
415	          if (d < radius * 0.3) {
416	            st.type[ti] = 0; st.flags[ti] = 0; st.wall[ti] = 83;
417	          } else if (d < radius * 0.8 && st.wall[ti] !== 83) {
418	            st.flags[ti] = 1; st.type[ti] = CRIMSTONE;
419	            if (d < radius * 0.6) st.wall[ti] = 83;
420	          }
421	        } else if (d < radius * 0.3 && st.flags[ti]) {
422	          st.type[ti] = 0; st.flags[ti] = 0; st.wall[ti] = 83;
423	        }
424	      }
425	    }
426	    px += vx; py += vy;
427	    vx += rng.int(-50, 50) * 0.01;
428	    vy += rng.int(-50, 50) * 0.01;
429	    vy = Math.max(0.25, Math.min(2, vy));
430	    vx = Math.max(-2, Math.min(2, vx));
431	    if (py > gs.worldSurface + 100) break;
432	    if (py > st.h - 10 || px < 5 || px > st.w - 5) break;
433	  }
434	  // ---- CrimVein:5-8 条血管从通道终点出发(Y 只向下/水平),终点记录为心位 ----
435	  const veinCount = rng.int(5, 8);
436	  const usedV: Array<[number, number]> = [];
437	  const hearts: Array<[number, number]> = [];
438	  for (let n = 0; n < veinCount; n++) {
439	    let vx2 = 0, vy2 = 0, tries2 = 0;
440	    for (;;) {
441	      vx2 = rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.15;
442	      vy2 = rng.int(0, 20) * 0.15;
443	      if (Math.abs(vx2) + Math.abs(vy2) >= 1.5
444	        && usedV.every(([ux, uy]) => Math.abs(ux - vx2) >= 0.75 || Math.abs(uy - vy2) >= 0.75)) break;
445	      if (++tries2 > 10000) break;
446	    }
447	    usedV.push([vx2, vy2]);
448	    hearts.push(crimVein(st, rng, px, py, vx2, vy2));
449	  }
450	  // ---- 地表囊群(CrimStart 末段:从入口点向上漂移的 25-35 半径球)----
451	  let sx = entryX + 0.0, sy = startY + 0.0;
452	  let rise = rng.next() * 6;
453	  const pocketR = rng.int(25, 34);
454	  for (let n = 0; n < 50; n++) {
455	    if (rise > 0) {
456	      const step = rng.int(10, 29) * 0.01;
457	      rise -= step;
458	      sy -= step;
459	    }
460	    const cx = Math.floor(sx) + rng.int(-2, 2);
461	    const cy = Math.floor(sy) + rng.int(-2, 2);
462	    for (let x = cx - Math.floor(pocketR / 2); x < cx + pocketR / 2; x++) {
463	      for (let y = cy - Math.floor(pocketR / 2); y < cy + pocketR / 2; y++) {
464	        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
465	        const d = Math.hypot((x - cx) * (1 + rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.01), (y - cy) * (1 + rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.01));
466	        const ti = st.idx(x, y);
467	        if (d < pocketR * 0.2 * (rng.int(90, 110) * 0.01)) {

479	  crimEnt(st, rng, entryX, Math.floor(sy), vx >= 0 ? -1 : 1);

484	      for (let y = hy - Math.floor(r1 / 2); y < hy + r1 / 2; y++) {

506	/** CrimVein(L45724)1:1:血管走——核心 0.2R 挖空(墙83)/壳 0.5R 猩红石;曼哈顿行程 100-150 */
507	function crimVein(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, sx: number, sy: number, ivx: number, ivy: number): [number, number] {
508	  let radius = rng.int(15, 25);
509	  const trip = rng.int(100, 149) - (ivy < 0 ? 25 : 0);
510	  let px = sx, py = sy;
511	  let vx = ivx, vy = ivy;
512	  while (Math.abs(px - sx) + Math.abs(py - sy) <= trip) {
513	    radius += rng.int(-50, 50) * 0.02;
514	    radius = Math.max(15, Math.min(25, radius));
515	    const x0 = Math.floor(px - radius / 2), x1 = Math.floor(px + radius / 2);
516	    const y0 = Math.floor(py - radius / 2), y1 = Math.floor(py + radius / 2);
517	    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
518	      for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
519	        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
520	        const d = Math.hypot(x - px, y - py);
521	        const ti = st.idx(x, y);
522	        if (d < radius * 0.2) {
523	          st.type[ti] = 0; st.flags[ti] = 0; st.wall[ti] = 83;
524	        } else if (d < radius * 0.5 && st.wall[ti] !== 83) {
525	          st.flags[ti] = 1; st.type[ti] = CRIMSTONE;
526	          if (d < radius * 0.4) st.wall[ti] = 83;
527	        }
528	      }
529	    }
530	    vx += rng.int(-50, 50) * 0.05;
531	    vy += rng.int(-50, 50) * 0.05;
532	    vx = Math.max(ivx - 0.75, Math.min(ivx + 0.75, vx));
533	    vy = Math.max(ivy - 0.75, Math.min(ivy + 0.75, vy));
534	    px += vx; py += vy;
535	    if (py > st.h - 30 || py < 5 || px < 5 || px > st.w - 5) break;
536	  }
537	  return [Math.floor(px), Math.floor(py)];
538	}

540	/** CrimEnt(L45689)1:1:水平入口隧道,只贯穿猩红石,连续 20 步无肉即停 */
541	function crimEnt(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x0: number, y0: number, dir: number): void {
542	  let radius = rng.int(6, 10);
543	  const vx = 2 * -dir;
544	  let vy = rng.int(-20, -1) * 0.01;
545	  let px = x0 + 0.0, py = y0 + 0.0;
546	  let misses = 0;
547	  while (misses < 20) {
548	    misses++;
549	    radius += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.02;
550	    radius = Math.max(6, Math.min(10, radius));
551	    const bx0 = Math.floor(px - radius / 2), bx1 = Math.floor(px + radius / 2);
552	    const by0 = Math.floor(py - radius / 2), by1 = Math.floor(py + radius / 2);
553	    for (let x = bx0; x < bx1; x++) {
554	      for (let y = by0; y < by1; y++) {
555	        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
556	        const ti = st.idx(x, y);
557	        if (st.flags[ti] && st.type[ti] === CRIMSTONE && Math.hypot(x - px, y - py) < radius * 0.5) {
558	          st.type[ti] = 0; st.flags[ti] = 0;
559	          misses = 0;
560	        }
561	      }
562	    }
563	    px += vx; py += vy;
564	  }
565	}
```

```
1	// 确定性随机数：mulberry32 + 2D hash（世界生成用）
2	export class RNG {
3	  private s: number;
4	  constructor(seed: number) {
5	    this.s = seed >>> 0;
6	  }
7	  next(): number {
8	    // mulberry32
9	    this.s = (this.s + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;
10	    let t = this.s;
11	    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
12	    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
13	    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
14	  }
15	  range(min: number, max: number): number {
16	    return min + this.next() * (max - min);
17	  }
18	  int(min: number, max: number): number {
19	    return Math.floor(this.range(min, max + 1));
20	  }
21	  chance(p: number): boolean {
22	    return this.next() < p;
23	  }
24	  pick<T>(arr: T[]): T {
25	    return arr[Math.floor(this.next() * arr.length)];
26	  }
27	}
28	
29	export function hashString(str: string): number {
30	  let h = 2166136261 >>> 0;
31	  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
32	    h ^= str.charCodeAt(i);
33	    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);
34	  }
35	  return h >>> 0;
36	}
37	
38	// 2D 整数 hash → [0,1)，无状态确定性噪声
39	export function hash2(x: number, y: number, seed: number): number {
40	  let h = seed >>> 0;
41	  h = Math.imul(h ^ (x | 0), 0x27d4eb2d);
42	  h = Math.imul(h ^ (y | 0), 0x85ebca6b);
43	  h ^= h >>> 15;
44	  h = Math.imul(h, 0x2c1b3c6d);
45	  h ^= h >>> 12;
46	  return (h >>> 0) / 4294967296;
47	}
```

```
44	      origin.X -= this._sourceMagmaMap.GetLength(0) / 2;
45	      origin.Y -= this._sourceMagmaMap.GetLength(1) / 2;
46	      this.BuildMagmaMap(origin);
47	      Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Rectangle effectedMapArea;
48	      this.SimulatePressure(out effectedMapArea);
49	      this.PlaceGranite(origin, effectedMapArea);
50	      this.CleanupTiles(origin, effectedMapArea);
51	      this.PlaceDecorations(origin, effectedMapArea);
52	      structures.AddStructure(effectedMapArea, 8);
53	      return true;
54	    }
55	
56	    private void BuildMagmaMap(Point tileOrigin)
57	    {
58	      this._sourceMagmaMap = new GraniteBiome.Magma[200, 200];
59	      this._targetMagmaMap = new GraniteBiome.Magma[200, 200];
60	      for (int index1 = 0; index1 < this._sourceMagmaMap.GetLength(0); ++index1)
61	      {
62	        for (int index2 = 0; index2 < this._sourceMagmaMap.GetLength(1); ++index2)
63	        {
64	          int i = index1 + tileOrigin.X;
65	          int j = index2 + tileOrigin.Y;
66	          this._sourceMagmaMap[index1, index2] = GraniteBiome.Magma.CreateEmpty(WorldGen.SolidTile(i, j, false) ? 4f : 1f);
67	          this._targetMagmaMap[index1, index2] = this._sourceMagmaMap[index1, index2];
68	        }
69	      }
70	    }
71	
72	    private void SimulatePressure(out Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Rectangle effectedMapArea)
73	    {
74	      int length1 = this._sourceMagmaMap.GetLength(0);
75	      int length2 = this._sourceMagmaMap.GetLength(1);
76	      int index1 = length1 / 2;
77	      int index2 = length2 / 2;
78	      int num1 = index1;
79	      int min1 = num1;
80	      int num2 = index2;
81	      int min2 = num2;
82	      for (int index3 = 0; index3 < 300; ++index3)
83	      {
84	        for (int index4 = num1; index4 <= min1; ++index4)
85	        {
86	          for (int index5 = num2; index5 <= min2; ++index5)
87	          {
88	            GraniteBiome.Magma sourceMagma1 = this._sourceMagmaMap[index4, index5];
89	            if (sourceMagma1.IsActive)
90	            {
91	              float num3 = 0.0f;
92	              Vector2 zero = Vector2.Zero;
93	              for (int index6 = -1; index6 <= 1; ++index6)
94	              {
95	                for (int index7 = -1; index7 <= 1; ++index7)
96	                {
97	                  if (index6 != 0 || index7 != 0)
98	                  {
99	                    Vector2 normalisedVector = GraniteBiome._normalisedVectors[(index6 + 1) * 3 + (index7 + 1)];
100	                    GraniteBiome.Magma sourceMagma2 = this._sourceMagmaMap[index4 + index6, index5 + index7];
101	                    if ((double) sourceMagma1.Pressure > 0.00999999977648258 && !sourceMagma2.IsActive)
102	                    {
103	                      if (index6 == -1)
104	                        num1 = Utils.Clamp<int>(index4 + index6, 1, num1);
105	                      else
106	                        min1 = Utils.Clamp<int>(index4 + index6, min1, length1 - 2);
107	                      if (index7 == -1)
108	                        num2 = Utils.Clamp<int>(index5 + index7, 1, num2);
109	                      else
110	                        min2 = Utils.Clamp<int>(index5 + index7, min2, length2 - 2);
111	                      this._targetMagmaMap[index4 + index6, index5 + index7] = sourceMagma2.ToFlow();
112	                    }
113	                    float pressure = sourceMagma2.Pressure;
114	                    num3 += pressure;
115	                    zero += pressure * normalisedVector;
116	                  }
117	                }
118	              }
119	              float num4 = num3 / 8f;
120	              if ((double) num4 > (double) sourceMagma1.Resistance)
121	              {
122	                float num5 = zero.Length() / 8f;
123	                float pressure = Math.Max(0.0f, (float) ((double) Math.Max(num4 - num5 - sourceMagma1.Pressure, 0.0f) + (double) num5 + (double) sourceMagma1.Pressure * 0.875) - sourceMagma1.Resistance);
124	                this._targetMagmaMap[index4, index5] = GraniteBiome.Magma.CreateFlow(pressure, Math.Max(0.0f, sourceMagma1.Resistance - pressure * 0.02f));
125	              }
126	            }
127	          }
128	        }
129	        if (index3 < 2)
130	          this._targetMagmaMap[index1, index2] = GraniteBiome.Magma.CreateFlow(25f, 0.0f);
131	        Utils.Swap<GraniteBiome.Magma[,]>(ref this._sourceMagmaMap, ref this._targetMagmaMap);
132	      }
133	      effectedMapArea = new Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Rectangle(num1, num2, min1 - num1 + 1, min2 - num2 + 1);
134	    }
135	
136	    private bool ShouldUseLava(Point tileOrigin)
137	    {
138	      int length1 = this._sourceMagmaMap.GetLength(0);
139	      int length2 = this._sourceMagmaMap.GetLength(1);
140	      int num1 = length1 / 2;
141	      int num2 = length2 / 2;
142	      if (tileOrigin.Y + num2 <= WorldGen.lavaLine - 30)
143	        return false;
144	      for (int index1 = -50; index1 < 50; ++index1)
145	      {
146	        for (int index2 = -50; index2 < 50; ++index2)
147	        {
148	          if (GenBase._tiles[tileOrigin.X + num1 + index1, tileOrigin.Y + num2 + index2].active())
149	          {
150	            switch (GenBase._tiles[tileOrigin.X + num1 + index1, tileOrigin.Y + num2 + index2].type)
151	            {
152	              case 147:
153	              case 161:
154	              case 162:
155	              case 163:
156	              case 200:
157	                return false;
158	              default:
159	                continue;
160	            }
161	          }
162	        }
163	      }
164	      return true;
165	    }
166	
167	    private void PlaceGranite(Point tileOrigin, Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Rectangle magmaMapArea)
168	    {
169	      bool flag = this.ShouldUseLava(tileOrigin);
170	      ushort type = 368;
171	      ushort num = 180;
172	      if (WorldGen.drunkWorldGen)
173	      {
174	        type = (ushort) 367;
175	        num = (ushort) 178;
176	      }
177	      for (int left = magmaMapArea.Left; left < magmaMapArea.Right; ++left)
178	      {
179	        for (int top = magmaMapArea.Top; top < magmaMapArea.Bottom; ++top)
180	        {
181	          GraniteBiome.Magma sourceMagma = this._sourceMagmaMap[left, top];
182	          if (sourceMagma.IsActive)
183	          {
184	            Tile tile = GenBase._tiles[tileOrigin.X + left, tileOrigin.Y + top];
185	            if ((double) Math.Max(1f - Math.Max(0.0f, (float) (Math.Sin((double) (tileOrigin.Y + top) * 0.400000005960464) * 0.699999988079071 + 1.20000004768372) * (float) (0.200000002980232 + 0.5 / Math.Sqrt((double) Math.Max(0.0f, sourceMagma.Pressure - sourceMagma.Resistance)))), sourceMagma.Pressure / 15f) > 0.349999994039536 + (WorldGen.SolidTile(tileOrigin.X + left, tileOrigin.Y + top, false) ? 0.0 : 0.5))
186	            {
187	              if (TileID.Sets.Ore[(int) tile.type])
188	                tile.ResetToType(tile.type);
189	              else
190	                tile.ResetToType(type);
191	              tile.wall = num;
192	            }
193	            else if ((double) sourceMagma.Resistance < 0.00999999977648258)
194	            {
195	              WorldUtils.ClearTile(tileOrigin.X + left, tileOrigin.Y + top, false);
196	              tile.wall = num;
197	            }
198	            if (tile.liquid > (byte) 0 & flag)
199	              tile.liquidType(1);
200	          }
201	        }
202	      }
203	    }
204	
205	    private void CleanupTiles(Point tileOrigin, Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Rectangle magmaMapArea)
206	    {
207	      ushort num1 = 180;
208	      if (WorldGen.drunkWorldGen)
209	        num1 = (ushort) 178;
210	      List<Point16> point16List = new List<Point16>();
211	      for (int left = magmaMapArea.Left; left < magmaMapArea.Right; ++left)
212	      {
213	        for (int top = magmaMapArea.Top; top < magmaMapArea.Bottom; ++top)
214	        {
215	          if (this._sourceMagmaMap[left, top].IsActive)
216	          {
217	            int num2 = 0;
218	            int num3 = left + tileOrigin.X;
219	            int num4 = top + tileOrigin.Y;
220	            if (WorldGen.SolidTile(num3, num4, false))
221	            {
222	              for (int index1 = -1; index1 <= 1; ++index1)
223	              {
224	                for (int index2 = -1; index2 <= 1; ++index2)
225	                {
226	                  if (WorldGen.SolidTile(num3 + index1, num4 + index2, false))
227	                    ++num2;
228	                }
229	              }
230	              if (num2 < 3)
231	                point16List.Add(new Point16(num3, num4));
232	            }
233	          }
234	        }
235	      }
236	      foreach (Point16 point16 in point16List)
237	      {
238	        int x = (int) point16.X;
239	        int y = (int) point16.Y;
240	        WorldUtils.ClearTile(x, y, true);
241	        GenBase._tiles[x, y].wall = num1;
242	      }
243	      point16List.Clear();
244	    }
245	
246	    private void PlaceDecorations(Point tileOrigin, Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Rectangle magmaMapArea)
247	    {
248	      FastRandom fastRandom1 = new FastRandom(Main.ActiveWorldFileData.Seed).WithModifier(65440UL);
249	      for (int left = magmaMapArea.Left; left < magmaMapArea.Right; ++left)
250	      {
251	        for (int top = magmaMapArea.Top; top < magmaMapArea.Bottom; ++top)
252	        {
253	          GraniteBiome.Magma sourceMagma = this._sourceMagmaMap[left, top];
254	          int index1 = left + tileOrigin.X;
255	          int index2 = top + tileOrigin.Y;
256	          if (sourceMagma.IsActive)
257	          {
258	            WorldUtils.TileFrame(index1, index2, false);
259	            WorldGen.SquareWallFrame(index1, index2, true);
260	            FastRandom fastRandom2 = fastRandom1.WithModifier(index1, index2);
261	            if (fastRandom2.Next(8) == 0 && GenBase._tiles[index1, index2].active())
262	            {
263	              if (!GenBase._tiles[index1, index2 + 1].active())
264	                WorldGen.PlaceUncheckedStalactite(index1, index2 + 1, fastRandom2.Next(2) == 0, fastRandom2.Next(3), false);
265	              if (!GenBase._tiles[index1, index2 - 1].active())
266	                WorldGen.PlaceUncheckedStalactite(index1, index2 - 1, fastRandom2.Next(2) == 0, fastRandom2.Next(3), false);
267	            }
268	            if (fastRandom2.Next(2) == 0)
269	              Tile.SmoothSlope(index1, index2, true, false);
270	          }
271	        }
272	      }
273	    }
274	
275	    private struct Magma
276	    {
277	      public readonly float Pressure;
278	      public readonly float Resistance;
279	      public readonly bool IsActive;
280	
281	      private Magma(float pressure, float resistance, bool active)
282	      {
283	        this.Pressure = pressure;
284	        this.Resistance = resistance;
285	        this.IsActive = active;
286	      }
287	
288	      public GraniteBiome.Magma ToFlow()
289	      {
290	        return new GraniteBiome.Magma(this.Pressure, this.Resistance, true);
291	      }
292	
293	      public static GraniteBiome.Magma CreateFlow(float pressure, float resistance = 0.0f)
294	      {
295	        return new GraniteBiome.Magma(pressure, resistance, true);
296	      }
297	
298	      public static GraniteBiome.Magma CreateEmpty(float resistance = 0.0f)
299	      {
300	        return new GraniteBiome.Magma(0.0f, resistance, false);
301	      }
302	    }
303	  }
304	}
```

```
- 完全对齐:IceBiomePass 核心数值、JunglePass 全部主要数值、Mushroom ShroomPatch 主体、Corruption 裂隙触发/ChasmRunner/CrimEnt/CrimVein/心室/球囊。
```
